#### Art. 2.

Article 410, paragraph 2, of the Penal Code, inserted by the law of Art.

November 24, 1997 and amended by the law of November 28, 2000, dewetvan24november1997engewijzigdbijdewetvan28noveis supplemented as follows: ber2000,wordtaangevuldalsvolgt:

"In addition, in the case referred to in article 398, paragraph 1, the «Bovendien wordt de maximumstraf, in het geval bepaald in maximum penalty is increased to one year of imprisonment." artikel398,eerstelid,verhoogdtotgevangenisstrafvaneenjaar.»

#### Art. 3.

In article 223 of the Civil Code, the following paragraph is inserted Art. 3. In artikel 223 van het Burgerlijk Wetboek wordt tussen het between paragraphs 2 and 3: tweedeenhetderdelidhetvolgendelidingevoegd:

"If a spouse has committed against the other an act referred to in «Indieneenechtgenootzichtegenoverdeandereschuldiggemaakt articles375,398to400,402,403or405ofthePenalCode,orhasattempted heeftaaneenfeitalsbedoeldindeartikelen375,398tot400,402,403of tocommitanactreferredtoinarticles375,393,394or397ofthesame 405 van het Strafwetboek, of heeft gepoogd een feit te plegen als Code,orifthereareseriousindicationsofsuchbehavior,thevictim bedoeldindeartikelen375,393,394of397vanhetzelfdeWetboek,of spouse shall be granted, except in exceptional circumstances, the indien er ernstige aanwijzingen voor dergelijke gedragingen bestaan, enjoyment of the marital residence if he or she so requests." zal de echtgenoot die het slachtoffer is, behalve bij uitzonderlijke omstandigheden, het genot toegewezen krijgen van de echtelijke verblijfplaatsindienhijdaaromverzoekt.»

#### Art. 4.

In article 1447 of the same Code, inserted by the law of July 14, 1976, the following paragraph is inserted between the first and second paragraphs:

"Except in exceptional circumstances, the request made by the spouse who was the victim of an act referred to in articles 375, 398 to 400, 402, 403 or 405 of the Penal Code or of an attempt to commit an act referred to in articles 375, 393, 394 or 397 of the same Code, shall be granted, either when the other spouse has been convicted on that ground by a decision that has become res judicata and the divorce has been pronounced against him or her, or when the decision pronouncing the divorce is based in whole or in part on that act."

#### Art. 5.

Article 1479 of the same Code, inserted by the law of November 23, 1998, is completed by the following paragraph:

"If a legal cohabitant has committed against the other an act referred to in articles 375, 398 to 400, 402, 403 or 405 of the Penal Code or has attempted to commit an act referred to in articles 375, 393, 394 or 397 of the same Code, or if there are serious indications of such conduct, the latter shall be granted, except in exceptional circumstances, enjoyment of the common residence if he or she so requests."

#### Art. 6.

In article 1280 of the Judicial Code, amended by the laws of July 14, 1976, February 2, 1994, June 30, 1994 and May 20, 1997, the following paragraph is inserted between the sixth and seventh paragraphs:

"If a spouse has committed against the other an act referred to in articles 375, 398 to 400, 402, 403 or 405 of the Penal Code or has attempted to commit an act referred to in articles 375, 393, 394 or 397 of the same Code, or if there are serious indications of such conduct, the spouse who is the victim shall be granted, except in exceptional circumstances, enjoyment of the marital residence if he or she so requests."

F. 2003 — 570 [S − C − 2003/12034] F. 2003 — 570 [S − C − 2003/12034]

JANUARY 20, 2003. — Law on strengthening JANUARY 20, 2003. — Law on strengthening legislation against racism (1) legislation against racism (1)

ALBERT II, King of the Belgians, ALBERT II, King of the Belgians, To all, present and to come, Greetings. To all, present and to come, Greetings.

The Houses have adopted and We sanction the following: The Houses have adopted and We sanction the following: CHAPTER I. — General provision CHAPTER I. — General provision

#### Article 1.

This law regulates a matter referred to in article 78 of the This law regulates a matter referred to in article 78 of the

Constitution. Constitution.

## CHAPTER II. — Amendments to the law of July 30, 1981 CHAPTER II. — Amendments to the law of July 30, 1981

aimed at suppressing certain acts inspired by racism or xenophobia aimed at suppressing certain acts inspired by racism or xenophobia

#### Art. 2.

In Article 1 of the law of 30 July 1981 aimed at repressing certain acts inspired by racism or xenophobia, modified by the law of 12 April 1994, the following amendments are made:

1° The following paragraph is inserted between paragraphs 1 and 2:

"Any conduct consisting of ordering anyone to practice discrimination against a person, a group, a community or their members is considered discrimination within the meaning of this law.";

2° in the former paragraph 2, which becomes paragraph 3, in 1°, the words "by reason of his race" are replaced by the words "by reason of an alleged race";

3° in the same paragraph, in 2°, the word "rassenscheiding" in the Dutch text is replaced by the word "segregatie" and the words "het ras" are replaced by the words "een zogenaamd ras";

4° in the same paragraph, in 3°, the words "of his race, his colour, his descent, his origin, or his nationality" are replaced by the words "of an alleged race, his colour, his descent or his national or ethnic origin";

7024 MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD

5° in the same paragraph, in 4°, the words "of the race, the colour, the descent, the origin, or the nationality" are replaced by the words "of an alleged race, the colour, the descent or the national or ethnic origin".

#### Art. 3.

In Article 2 of the same law, replaced by the law of 12 April 1994 and modified by the law of 26 June 2000, the following amendments are made:

1° in paragraph 1, the words "of his race, his colour, his descent, his origin or his nationality" are replaced by the words "of an alleged race, his colour, his descent or his national or ethnic origin";

2° in paragraph 2, the words "of the race, the colour, the descent, the origin or the nationality" are replaced by the words "of an alleged race, his colour, descent, or the national or ethnic origin".

#### Art. 4.

In Article 2bis of the same law, inserted by the law of 12 April 1994, the following amendments are made:

1° in paragraph 1, the words "of his race" are replaced by the words "of an alleged race";

2° the following paragraph is inserted between paragraphs 1 and 2:

"The same penalties are applied when the discrimination against a group, a community or their members is based on the alleged race, the colour, the descent or the national or ethnic origin of these members or some of them."

#### Art. 5.

In Article 4 of the same law, modified by the law of 12 April 1994, the following amendments are made:

1° in paragraph 1, the words "of his race, his colour, his descent, his origin or his nationality" are replaced by the words "of an alleged race, his colour, his descent or his national or ethnic origin";

2° in paragraph 2, the words "of the race the colour, the descent, the origin or the nationality" are replaced by the words "of an alleged race, the colour, the descent or the national or ethnic origin".

#### Art. 6.

In the same law, an Article 5ter is inserted, worded as follows:

"Art. 5ter. Without prejudice to the powers of judicial police officers, the officials designated by the King shall monitor compliance with this law and its implementing decrees.

These officials shall exercise this monitoring in accordance with the provisions of the law of 16 November 1972 concerning labour inspection.".

## CHAPTER III.

Amendments to the law of 15 February 1993 creating a Centre for Equal Opportunities and the Fight against Racism

#### Art. 7.

In Article 2 of the law of 15 February 1993 creating a Centre for equal opportunities and combating racism, amended by the law of 13 April 1995, the following sentence is inserted before the sentence "The Centre is furthermore tasked with stimulating the fight against trafficking in human beings": "The Centre also has the mission to ensure respect for the fundamental rights of foreigners, to inform the public authorities about the nature and extent of migratory flows and to develop consultation and dialogue with all public and private actors concerned with the reception and integration policies for immigrants."

#### Art. 8.

In Article 3 of the same law, amended by the law of 13 April 1995, the following amendments are made:

1° A 4°bis is inserted, worded as follows:

«4bis. to receive, within the limits of its mission defined in Article 2, complaints, to process them and to accomplish any mediation mission that it deems useful, without prejudice to the powers of the federal ombudsmen;».

2° The article is completed as follows:

«9° to collect and publish, without possibility of identification of the parties involved, statistical data and case law decisions useful for the evaluation of the application of the aforementioned law of 30 July 1981 and of the law of 6 January 2003 aimed at combating discrimination and amending the law of 15 February 1993 creating a Centre for equal opportunities and combating racism;

10° to ask the competent authority, when the Centre invokes facts that allow to presume the existence of discrimination, as referred to in the aforementioned laws of 30 July 1981 and 6 January 2003, to inform itself and to keep the Centre informed of the results of the analysis of the facts in question. The authorities inform the Centre in a reasoned manner of the follow-up given thereto.

#### Art. 9.

In Article 4 of the same law, the following paragraph is inserted between paragraphs 1 and 2: "The Minister of Justice communicates annually to the Centre the judicial statistics relating to the application of the aforementioned laws of 30 July 1981 and 6 January 2003, as well as the judicial decisions taken in application of these laws, without possibility of identification of the parties involved."

## CHAPTER IV.—Amendment of the law of 13 May 1999 containing the disciplinary statute of members of the personnel of the police services

#### Art. 10.

Article 26 of the law of 13 May 1999 containing the disciplinary statute of members of the personnel of the police services is completed by the following paragraphs:

"When the Centre for equal opportunities and combating racism communicates to the Standing Committee for the control of the police services or to the General Inspectorate of the federal and local police services facts suggesting discriminatory treatment within the meaning of the law of 30 July 1981 aimed at suppressing certain acts inspired by racism or xenophobia or of the law of 6 January 2003 aimed at combating discrimination and amending the law of 15 February 1993 creating a Centre for equal opportunities and combating racism, the chairman of the Standing Committee or the Inspector General, as the case may be, has an investigation conducted concerning these facts, informs the competent authority and refers the matter to the disciplinary or judicial authority if the facts justify it. The chairman of the Standing Committee or the Inspector General, as the case may be, informs the Centre of the follow-up given to his steps and in particular of the follow-up that the disciplinary or judicial authority has given to the examination of the facts.

If the Standing Committee or the General Inspectorate is seized of facts that suggest discriminatory treatment within the meaning of the aforementioned laws, the chairman of the Standing Committee or the Inspector General, as the case may be, informs the Centre for equal opportunities and combating racism without communicating the identity of the parties concerned. He also informs the Centre of the follow-up that the disciplinary or judicial authority has given to the examination of the facts."

#### Art. 11.

This law enters into force on the day of its publication in the Belgian Official Gazette.

We promulgate this law, order that it be sealed with the State seal and published in the Belgian Official Gazette.

Given in Brussels, 20 January 2003.

ALBERT

By the King:

The Prime Minister,

G. VERHOFSTADT

The Minister of Employment,

Ms L. ONKELINX

Seen and sealed with the State seal:

The Minister of Justice,

M. VERWILGHEN

7026 MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD

FEDERAL PUBLIC SERVICE ECONOMY, SMEs, SELF-EMPLOYED AND ENERGY

F. 2003 — 571 [C − 2003/11040] N. 2003 — 571 [C − 2003/11040]

20 JANUARY 2003. — Royal decree establishing the programme, conditions and jury for the practical aptitude examination of certified accountants and certified accountants-tax advisers

ALBERT II, King of the Belgians,

To all, present and to come, Greetings.

Considering the law of 22 April 1999 on the accounting and tax professions, in particular Article 51;

Considering the opinion of the High Council for Economic Professions of 20 June 2001;

Considering opinion No. 32.902/3 of the Council of State, given on 24 September 2002;

On the proposal of Our Minister of Telecommunications and Public Enterprises and Holdings, in charge of the Self-Employed,

We have decreed and decree:

CHAPTER I. — Definitions

#### Article 1.

For the application of this decree, the following is understood by:

1° the law: the law of 22 April 1999 on the accounting and tax professions;

2° the Institute: the Professional Institute of Certified Accountants and Tax Advisers, created by Article 43 of the law;

3° the framework law: the framework law of 1 March 1976 regulating the protection of professional titles and the practice of intellectual service-providing professions;

4° the Chambers: the Executive Chambers, referred to in Article 6, § 3, of the framework law;

5° the Chambers of Appeal: the Chambers of Appeal, referred to in Article 6,

#### § 3, of the framework law;

6° the Council: the National Council, referred to in Article 6, § 3, of the framework law;

7° the Bureau: the Bureau of the Professional Institute of Certified Accountants and Tax Advisers, referred to in Article 34 of the royal decree of 27 November 1985 determining the rules of organisation and operation of the professional institutes created for intellectual service-providing professions;

8° the Internship Commission: the examination jury referred to in Article 51 of the law and in Chapter IV of this decree;

9° the practical aptitude examination: the examination provided for in Article 51 of the law.

## CHAPTER II. — Programme of the practical aptitude examination

#### Art. 2.

The practical aptitude examination has the purpose of verifying the capacity of the candidate, at the end of his internship period, to apply his theoretical knowledge to the practice of the profession of certified accountant or certified accountant-tax adviser and his ability to exercise the profession in compliance with the laws and rules of professional ethics.

#### Art. 3.

The practical aptitude examination comprises a written test and an equivalent oral test, directly or indirectly covering the following subjects:

1˚ general accounting;

2˚ legislation relating to accounting and annual accounts of enterprises;

3˚ general principles of financial management;

4˚ preparation, analysis and critical review of annual accounts;

5˚ organization of accounting and administrative services of enterprises;

6˚ value added tax;

7˚ tax on natural persons;

8˚ corporate tax;

9˚ tax procedures;

10˚ company law and legislation relating to enterprises in difficulty;

11˚ principles of registration and succession duties;

12˚ principles relating to customs and excise duties;

13˚ principles of labor law and social security;

14˚ professional ethics of certified accountants and certified tax accountants.

#### Art. 4.

#### §1. The written test of the aptitude examination for access to the title of certified accountant or certified tax accountant consists of solving one or more practical cases relating to the subjects referred to in Article 3.

#### §2. In the written test of the aptitude examination for access to the title of certified tax accountant, a more in-depth knowledge of the subjects referred to in Article 3, 6˚, 7˚, 8˚, 9˚, 11˚ and 12˚ will be required.

#### Art. 5.

The oral test includes a discussion of the written test and an examination on the practice of the function, the assignments, the liability and the professional ethics of certified accountants and certified tax accountants.

Only candidates who have obtained at least fifty percent of the points in the written test are admitted to the oral test.

Candidates for the title of certified tax accountant must also have obtained at least sixty percent of the points in the written test relating to the subjects mentioned in Article 4, §2, to be admitted to the oral test.

## CHAPTER III.—Conditions relating to admission and organization of the practical aptitude examination

#### Art. 6.

The candidate who wishes to participate in the practical aptitude examination addresses a written request to the Internship Commission, referred to in Chapter IV.

#### Art. 7.

The candidate is admitted to the practical aptitude examination as soon as he has completed his internship in accordance with the legal and regulatory provisions, as well as the directives issued by the Council, which must be respected by the trainee accountant or trainee tax accountant during his internship.

The Institute notifies in writing within a period of one month the trainee who has submitted a written request, as referred to in Article 6, of the date of the next practical aptitude examination. If the trainee does not meet the conditions to participate in the practical aptitude examination, he is informed in writing. He may submit a new request, in accordance with Article 6, as soon as he meets the access conditions referred to in the first paragraph.

#### Art. 8.

The practical aptitude examination is organized at least twice a year by the Institute and may be taken in one of the three national languages at the candidate's choice.

#### Art. 9.

The results are notified by the Institute to the candidate within three months of the practical aptitude examination and are added to his file.

#### Art. 10.

Candidates who, after the oral part, have obtained sixty percent of the points, have passed and are considered to have submitted their application for registration on the roll referred to in Article 46 of the Royal Decree of 27 November 1985 determining the rules of organization and operation of the professional institutes created for the intellectual professions providing services. The list of successful candidates is transmitted to the Chamber which will proceed with their registration on the roll of holders of the profession, in accordance with Article 8, §1, 1˚, of the Framework Act.

#### Art. 11.

If the candidate has not succeeded in accordance with article 10, he may submit a new application in writing, in accordance with article 6, in order to be admitted to the next practical aptitude examination, until the expiry of the maximum internship period of 36 months referred to in article 51 of the law.

## CHAPTERIV.—TheInternshipCommission/Theexaminationjury

#### Art. 12.

An Internship Commission is established within the Institute.

#### Art. 12.

This Internship Commission acts as examination jury, as referred to in article 51 of the law, and is responsible for conducting the practical aptitude examination organized by the Institute.

#### Art. 13.

In the exercise of its mission, the Internship Commission has the most extensive means of supervision and control. It may delegate to one or more of its members the tasks entrusted to it.

7028 MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD

#### Art. 14.

§ 1. The Internship Commission consists of twenty active members, including the president and vice-president of the Institute. Ten members are Dutch-speaking and 10 members are French-speaking. The active members are, on the proposal of the Bureau, appointed by the Council for a renewable term of four years. This takes place at the second meeting of the newly constituted Council following the elections. The appointments of the members of the Internship Commission are subject to the approval of the Minister responsible for the Middle Classes.

#### § 2. The president and vice-president of the Institute are also

president and vice-president of the Internship Commission.

#### § 3.

Where appropriate, the Council may, on the proposal of the Bureau, appoint substitute members whose term of office expires at the same time as that of the active members appointed in accordance with § 1. Substitute members may only attend meetings of the Internship Commission when they are called upon to replace an active member.

#### Art. 15.

§ 1. There is incompatibility between the mandate of member of the Internship Commission and the mandate of:

1° Active or substitute member of the Chamber;

2° Active or substitute member of the Appeal Chamber.

#### § 2.

With the exception of the president and the vice-president, the Internship Commission may comprise at most five Dutch-speaking and five French-speaking members who are also members of the Council.

#### § 3.

At least one French-speaking and one Dutch-speaking member must be professors or lecturers at a university, an institution of higher education at university level or an institution of higher economic education.

#### § 4. One of the members of the Internship Commission must have

sufficient knowledge of the German language.

#### § 5. The other members of the Internship Commission must be

members of the Institute or persons chosen on the basis of their specific knowledge of matters relevant to the profession.

#### Art. 16.

§ 1. The Internship Commission meets at the invitation of its president or, in his absence, its vice-president. The member who is prevented from attending notifies the president in due time.

#### § 2.

The meetings of the Internship Commission are chaired by the president or, in his absence, by the vice-president. If both are absent, the meeting is chaired by the oldest member present. The acting president has the same prerogatives as the president.

#### § 3.

The Internship Commission deliberates by linguistic role. It may only validly deliberate when at least six members of each linguistic role are present. Decisions are taken by a simple majority of votes. In the event of a tied vote, that of the president is decisive.

the practical aptitude examination referred to in § 1, which shall consist of an of an oral test conducted by the Traineeship Commit-oral test. tee.

ALBERT II, King of the Belgians, ALBERT II, King of the Belgians, To all, present and to come, Greetings. To all, present and to come, Our Greetings. Having regard to Article 26 of the law of 24 December 2002 modifying Having regard to Article 26 of the law of 24 December 2002 amending the regime of companies in respect of income taxes and establishing a the company regime in respect of income taxes and establishing a system of advance decision in tax matters; system of advance decisions in tax matters; Having regard to the opinion of the Inspector of Finance, given on Having regard to the opinion of the Inspector of Finance given on 5 December 2002; 5 December 2002;

Having regard to the agreement of the Minister of Budget, given on Having regard to the agreement of the Minister of Budget of 5 December 2002; 5 December 2002;

Having regard to the urgency motivated by the fact: Having regard to the urgency motivated by the fact: — that the provisions of the present decree are taken in application of — that the provisions of this decree are taken in execution of Article 26 of the law of 24 December 2002 modifying the regime of Article 26 of the law of 24 December 2002 amending the company companies in respect of income taxes and establishing a system of regime in respect of income taxes and establishing a system of advance decision in tax matters; advance decisions in tax matters; — that the provisions of Articles 20 to 28 of the same law apply from — that the provisions of Articles 20 to 28 of the same law have effect 1 January 2003; from 1 January 2003; — that it is therefore appropriate to bring the provisions of the — that it is therefore appropriate to bring the provisions of this present decree into force from the same date in order to ensure decree into force from the same date in order to ensure the functioning immediately the functioning of the system of advance decision in tax of the system of advance decisions in tax matters immediately; matters;

Having regard to the opinion of the Council of State, given on Having regard to the opinion of the Council of State given on 15 January 2003 in application of Article 84, paragraph 1, 2°, of the 15 January 2003 in application of Article 84, first paragraph, 2°, of the coordinated laws on the Council of State; coordinated laws on the Council of State;

On the proposal of Our Minister of Finance and the opinion of Our On the proposal of Our Minister of Finance, and on the opinion of Ministers who have deliberated in Council, Our Ministers who have deliberated in Council, We have decreed and decree: We have decreed and decree:

#### Article 1er.

Requests for advance decisions referred to in Article 20 of the law of 24 December 2002 amending the company regime in respect of income taxes and establishing a system of advance decisions in tax matters shall be the subject of an acknowledgment of receipt issued to the applicant within a period of five working days from the date of receipt of the request.

7038 MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD

#### Art. 2.

Any official of the Federal Public Service Finance may participate in the examination of the request.

The applicant may be heard on the initiative of the service or at their request.

#### Art. 3.

The Minister of Finance or their delegate shall designate the officials of the Federal Public Service Finance who are responsible for ruling on requests for advance decisions.

#### Art. 4.

The decision shall be notified to the applicant by registered mail.

#### Art. 5.

Advance decisions shall be published in the form of individual or collective summaries.

#### Art. 6.

The annual report referred to in Article 25 of the law of 24 December 2002 amending the company regime in respect of income taxes and establishing a system of advance decisions in tax matters shall be sent to the Minister of Finance no later than 30 June of the year following the one to which the report relates.

#### Art. 7.

The royal decree of 4 April 1995 on the Commission for Advance Tax Agreements, confirmed by the law of 2 June 1998, the royal decree of 3 May 1999 organising a system of advance decisions in tax matters, the ministerial decree of 27 May 1999 granting delegation to rule on requests for advance decisions in tax matters and the ministerial decree of 6 June 2000 on the appointment of members of the Commission for Advance Tax Agreements, amended by the ministerial decree of 4 January 2002, are repealed.

#### Art. 8.

#### § 1er. The royal decree of 4 April 1995 on the Commission for Advance Tax Agreements, confirmed by the law of 2 June 1998, and the ministerial decree of 6 June 2000 on the appointment of members of the Commission for Advance Tax Agreements, amended by the ministerial decree of 4 January 2002, as they existed before being repealed by Article 7, shall continue to apply to requests for advance tax agreements submitted before the date of entry into force of this decree in accordance with Article 18, § 3 to § 6, of the Code of Registration, Mortgage and Clerk's Fees, and Article 345 of the Income Tax Code 1992, as they existed before their repeal by Article 27 of the law of 24 December 2002 amending the company regime in respect of income taxes and establishing a system of advance decisions in tax matters, and Article 106, paragraph 2, of the Inheritance Tax Code, as it existed before being replaced by Article 28 of the same law.

The royal decree of 3 May 1999 organising a system of advance decisions in tax matters and the ministerial decree of 27 May 1999 granting delegation to rule on requests for advance decisions in tax matters, as they existed before being repealed by Article 7, shall continue to apply to requests for advance decisions submitted before the date of entry into force of this decree.

#### § 2.

The service of advance decisions established by Article 2 of the aforementioned royal decree of 3 May 1999 as it existed before being repealed by Article 7 shall continue to perform its duties until the decisions relating to the requests referred to in § 1, paragraphs 1 and 2, have been published and the reports relating to those activities have been submitted to the competent authority.

MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD 7039

#### § 3.

Articles 20 to 28 of the law of December 24, 2002 modifying the regime of companies in matters of income taxes and instituting a system of advance decision in tax matters, the provisions taken in application of article 22, paragraph 2 of the same law and the provisions of the present decree apply:

1° to applications for advance tax rulings that were submitted between January 1, 2003 and January 15, 2003 in accordance with article 18,

#### § 3

§ 3 to § 6, of the Code of registration, mortgage and court registry fees, and article 345 of the Income Tax Code 1992, as they existed before their repeal by article 27 of the law of December 24, 2002 modifying the regime of companies in matters of income taxes and instituting a system of advance decision in tax matters, and article 106, paragraph 2, of the Inheritance Tax Code, as it existed before being replaced by article 28 of the same law;

2° to applications for advance decisions that were submitted between January 1, 2003 and the date of publication of the present decree in accordance with the royal decree of May 3, 1999 establishing a system of advance decision in tax matters, as it existed before being repealed by article 7.

#### § 4.

By derogation from article 1, the acknowledgment of receipt may be issued until the fifth working day following the date of publication of the present decree for applications for advance decisions submitted between January 1, 2003 and that date of publication, as well as for the applications referred to in paragraph 3.

#### Art. 9.

The present decree takes effect on January 1, 2003.

#### Art. 10.

Our Minister of Finance is charged with the execution of the present decree.

Given in Brussels, January 30, 2003.

ALBERT

By the King:

The Minister of Finance,

JANUARY 21, 2003. — Royal decree modifying the royal decree of September 20, 2002 considering as a public calamity the abundant rainfall that occurred on July 30 and 31, 2002 on the territory of several municipalities, and delimiting the geographical extent of this calamity

ALBERT II, King of the Belgians,

To all, present and to come, Greetings.

Having regard to the law of July 12, 1976 concerning the compensation for certain damage caused to private property by natural disasters, in particular article 2, § 1, 1°, and § 2;

Having regard to the royal decree of September 20, 2002 considering as a public calamity the abundant rainfall that occurred on July 30 and 31, 2002 on the territory of several municipalities, and delimiting the geographical extent of this calamity;

Having regard to the requests of the Governors of the Provinces of Antwerp and Limburg to extend the geographical extent of the disaster area to the territory of other municipalities;

Having regard to the opinion of the Inspector of Finance given on December 11, 2002;

Having regard to the approval of Our Minister of Budget of January 16, 2003;

On the proposal of Our Minister of Interior and on the advice of Our Ministers who deliberated in Council,

We have decreed and decree:

#### Article 1er.

In article 2 of the royal decree of September 20, 2002 considering as a public calamity the abundant rainfall that occurred on July 30 and 31, 2002 on the territory of several municipalities, and delimiting the geographical extent of this calamity, the following modifications are made:

1° under the title "Province of Antwerp", the word "Balen" is added after the word "Arendonk";

2° under the title "Province of Limburg", the word "Hasselt" is inserted between the words "Beringen" and "Hechtel-Eksel".

#### Art. 2.

The present decree enters into force the day of its publication in the Belgian Official Gazette.

#### Art. 3.

Our Minister of the Interior is charged with the execution of this Order.

Given at Brussels, 21 January 2003.

ALBERT

By the King:

The Minister of the Interior,

A. DUQUESNE

F. 2003 — 579 [C − 2003/02023]

3 FEBRUARY 2003. — Royal Order containing various regulatory measures with a view to simplifying certain administrative control procedures

ALBERT II, King of the Belgians,
To all, present and to come, Greetings.
Having regard to Articles 37 and 107, paragraph 2, of the Constitution;
Having regard to the royal order of 12 May 1927 concerning the age of retirement of civil servants, employees and service staff of the State administrations, in particular Article 3, paragraph 2, replaced by the royal order of 13 September 1989;

Having regard to the royal order of 29 June 1973 establishing the remuneration regulations for the personnel of the federal public services, in particular Article 14, § 1, paragraph 2, inserted by the royal order of 27 March 2001 and amended by the royal order of 5 September 2002;

Having regard to the royal order of 8 August 1983 concerning the exercise of a higher function in the State administrations, in particular Article 7, § 3, amended by the royal order of 20 February 1989;
Having regard to the royal order of 3 September 2000 regulating the intervention of the State and certain public institutions in the transport costs of federal personnel members and amending the royal order of 20 April 1999 granting an allowance for the use of bicycles to personnel members of certain federal public services, in particular Articles 6, 9 and 10;

Having regard to the opinion of the Inspector of Finance, given on 19 August 2002;

Having regard to the agreement of Our Minister of the Budget, given on 29 August 2002;

Having regard to protocol No. 435 of 17 September 2002 of the Committee of federal, community and regional public services;
Having regard to protocol N-156 of 18 October 2002 of the Negotiating Committee for military personnel of the armed forces;
Having regard to the deliberation of the Council of Ministers on the request for an opinion to be given by the Council of State within a period not exceeding one month;
Having regard to opinion 34.443/1 of the Council of State, given on 12 December 2002, in application of Article 84, paragraph 1, 1°, of the coordinated laws on the Council of State;

On the proposal of Our Minister of the Civil Service and Modernization of the Administration and on the opinion of Our Ministers who deliberated in Council,

We have ordered and order:

#### Article 1er.

In Article 3, paragraph 2, of the royal order of 12 May 1927 concerning the age of retirement of civil servants, employees and service staff of the State administrations, replaced by the royal order of 13 September 1989, the words "and subject to the agreement of the Minister who has the civil service within his competence" are deleted.

#### Art. 2.

Article 14, § 1, paragraph 2, of the royal order of 29 June 1973 establishing the remuneration regulations for the personnel of the federal public services, inserted by the royal order of 27 March 2001, and amended by the royal order of 5 September 2002, is repealed.

#### Art. 3.

In Article 7, § 3, of the royal order of 8 August 1983 concerning the exercise of a higher function in the State administrations, amended by the royal order of 20 February 1989, the following amendments are made:
1° in paragraph 5, point b), the words "with the agreement of the Minister who has the civil service within his competence," are deleted;

2° paragraphs 6 and 7 are repealed.

#### Art. 4.

In Article 6 of the royal order of 3 September 2000 regulating the intervention of the State and certain public institutions in the transport costs of federal personnel members and amending the royal order of 20 April 1999 granting an allowance for the use of bicycles to personnel members of certain federal public services, the words "or as a complete network subscription" are inserted between the words "as an urban subscription to a network" and the words ", whatever the distance".

#### Art. 5.

Article 9 of the same Order is replaced by the following provision:

"Art. 9. - The member of staff who does not use public transport on a daily basis receives an intervention of 80% in the price of travel cards, on condition that the amount of that intervention is monthly less than the amount that would result from the use of a season ticket and that the transport ticket is used exclusively for the journey from home to the place of work."

#### Art. 6.

In article 10 of the same Order, the words "In application of article 9 and" are deleted.

#### Art. 7.

This Order enters into force on the first day of the month following the one in which it was published in the Belgian Official Gazette.

#### Art. 8.

Our Ministers and Our Secretaries of State are charged, each insofar as it concerns them, with the execution of this Order.

Given in Brussels, 3 February 2003.

ALBERT

By the King:

The Minister of Budget,

J. VANDELANOTTE

The Minister of the Civil Service and Modernization of the Administration,

L. VANDENBOSSCHE

5 DECEMBER 2002. — Order of the Government of the French Community amending the Order of 10 October 2002 relating to the procedure for controlling the practice of doping, and setting the entry into force of certain provisions of the decree of 8 March 2001 relating to the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and its prevention in the French Community

The Government of the French Community,

Having regard to the decree of 8 March 2001 relating to the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and its prevention in the French Community, in particular article 10;

Having regard to the Order of 10 October 2002 relating to the procedure for controlling the practice of doping, and setting the entry into force of certain provisions of the decree of 8 March 2001 relating to the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and its prevention in the French Community, in particular article 15;

Having regard to the opinion of the French-speaking Commission for the promotion of health in the practice of sport, given on 22 November 2002;

Having regard to the opinion of the Higher Council for Physical Education, Sport and Outdoor Life, given on 29 November 2002;

MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD 7045

Having regard to the urgency justified by the fact that the new list of prohibited substances and methods of the International Olympic Committee enters into force on 1 January 2003, and that the list applicable in the French Community must conform to that list;

On the proposal of the Minister with Health in her remit;

Having regard to the deliberation of the Government of the French Community of 5 December 2002;

Orders:

#### Article 1er.

The annex to the Order of 10 October 2002 relating to the procedure for controlling the practice of doping, and setting the entry into force of certain provisions of the decree of 8 March 2001 relating to the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and its prevention in the French Community, is replaced by the annex attached to this Order.

#### Art. 2. This Order shall enter into force on January 1, 2003.

Brussels, December 5, 2002.

For the Government of the French Community, The Minister of Youth Assistance and Health, Mrs. N. MARECHAL

Annex to the Order of October 10, 2002 concerning the procedure for controlling the practice of doping, and establishing the entry into force of the decree of March 8, 2001 concerning the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and its prevention in the French Community, as amended by the Order of December 5, 2002
List of prohibited substances and methods

I. PROHIBITED CLASSES OF SUBSTANCES

A. Stimulants

a. The prohibited substances belonging to class (A. a.) include the following examples, as well as their L and D isomers: amiphenazole, amphetamines, bromantan, caffeine*, carphedon, cocaine, ephedrines**, fencamfamine, mesocarb, pentetrazol, pipradol, ... and related substances.

* For caffeine, a concentration in urine exceeding 12 micrograms per milliliter will be considered a positive result.

** For ephedrine and methylephedrine, a concentration in urine exceeding 10 micrograms per milliliter will be considered a positive result. For cathine, a concentration in urine exceeding 5 micrograms per milliliter will be considered a positive result.

For phenylpropanolamine and pseudoephedrine, a concentration in urine exceeding 25 micrograms per milliliter will be considered a positive result.
NOTE: All imidazole preparations are acceptable for topical application. Vasoconstrictors may be administered with local anesthetic agents. Preparations for local use (for example nasal, ophthalmological, rectal) of adrenaline are permitted. Bupropion, synephrine and phenylephrine are permitted.

b. The prohibited substances belonging to class (A. b.) include the following examples as well as their L and D isomers:

Formoterol***, salbutamol***, salmeterol*** and terbutaline***

... and related substances

*** Substance permitted by inhalation only to prevent and/or treat asthma and exercise-induced asthma, provided that this authorization is prior, and issued by the competent medical authority, in application of the regulations of the sports federation or the international sports federation.

B. Narcotics

The prohibited substances belonging to class (B) include the following examples: buprenorphine, dextromoramide, diamorphine (heroin), methadone, morphine, pentazocine, pethidine, ... and related substances.

NOTE: Codeine, dextromethorphan, dextropropoxyphene, dihydrocodeine, diphenoxylate, ethylmorphine, pholcodine, propoxyphene and tramadol are permitted.

C. Anabolic agents

The prohibited substances belonging to class (C) include the following examples:
1. Anabolic androgenic steroids
a/ clostebol, fluoxymesterone, methandienone, metenolone, nandrolone, 19-norandrostenediol, 19-norandrostenedione, oxandrolone, stanozolol, ... and related substances.
b/ androstenediol, androstenedione, dehydroepiandrosterone (DHEA), dihydrotestosterone, testosterone*, ... and related substances.

* The presence of a testosterone (T)-epitestosterone (E) ratio exceeding six (6) in the urine of an athlete constitutes an infraction unless it is established that this ratio is due to a physiological or pathological condition, e.g. low excretion of epitestosterone, androgenic production from a tumor or enzymatic deficiencies.
In the case of a T/E ratio exceeding 6, the sports federation or the international sports federation may, in application of its regulations, have an examination carried out under the direction of its competent medical authority before the sample is declared positive. A complete report will be prepared; it will include, if applicable, a review of previous and subsequent tests as well as the results of endocrine tests. If previous tests are not available, the athlete must undergo an unannounced control at least once a month for three months.

The results of these examinations must be included in the report. In the absence of cooperation from the athlete, this will result in a declaration of a positive sample.

NOTES: Evidence obtained from metabolic profiles and/or the study of isotopic ratios may be used to draw definitive conclusions.

2. Other anabolic agents
clenbuterol, salbutamol*

7046 MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD

* For salbutamol, a urinary concentration of non-sulfated salbutamol exceeding 1000 nanograms per milliliter will be considered a positive result.

D. Diuretics

The prohibited substances belonging to class (D) include the following examples: acetazolamide, ethacrynic acid, bumetanide, chlorthalidone, furosemide, hydrochlorothiazide, mannitol*, mersalyl, spironolactone, triamterene, ... and related substances.

* Prohibited substance if injected intravenously.

E. Peptide hormones, mimetic substances and analogues

The prohibited substances belonging to class (E) include the following substances and their analogues as well as mimetic substances:

1. Chorionic gonadotrophin (hCG) in men only.
2. Pituitary and synthetic gonadotrophins in men only.

3. Corticotrophins (ACTH, tetracosactide).

4. Growth hormone (hGH).

5. Insulin-like growth factor (IGF-1) and all their respective release factors as well as their analogues.

6. Erythropoietin (EPO).

7. Insulin*.

* Insulin may be permitted only to treat athletes suffering from insulin-dependent diabetes, provided that this authorization is prior, and issued by the competent medical authority, according to the regulations of the sports federation or the international sports federation.
NOTE: The presence in the urine of an athlete of an abnormal concentration of an endogenous hormone belonging to class (E) or its diagnostic marker(s) constitutes an infraction unless it is proven that it is due to a physiological or pathological condition.

F. Agents having anti-estrogenic action.
Aromatase inhibitors, clomiphene, cyclofenil, tamoxifen are prohibited in men only.
G. Masking agents.

The prohibited substances of class (G) include the following examples: Diuretics, epitestosterone*, probenecid, plasma expanders (such as hydroxyethyl starch)
Masking agents are prohibited. These are products that have the capacity to hinder the excretion of prohibited substances or to conceal their presence in urine or other samples used in doping control.

* The presence of a concentration of epitestosterone exceeding 200 ng/mL in urine constitutes an infraction unless it is established that it is due to a physiological condition. Isotope ratio mass spectrometry (IRMS) may be used to draw definitive conclusions. If the results of the IRMS are not conclusive, the competent medical authority will conduct an investigation before the sample is declared positive.

II. PROHIBITED METHODS

The following methods are prohibited:

A. Increase in oxygen transfer

Blood doping: this is the administration of autologous, homologous or heterologous blood, or red blood cell products, other than for legitimate therapeutic purposes.

The administration of products that increase the uptake, transport or release of oxygen, such as products based on modified hemoglobins including, but not limited to, bovine and cross-linked hemoglobins, microencapsulated hemoglobin-based products, perfluorocarbons and RSR13.
B. Pharmacological, chemical and physical manipulation

Pharmacological, chemical and physical manipulation is the use of substances and methods, masking agents (see I.G), which alter, attempt to alter or may reasonably be expected to alter the integrity and validity of urine samples used in doping controls, such as, but not limited to, catheterization, substitution and/or alteration of urine, inhibition of renal excretion and modification of measurements carried out on testosterone and epitestosterone (see I.G).

C. Gene doping
Gene or genetic doping is defined as the non-therapeutic use of genes, genetic elements and/or cells that have the capacity to enhance athletic performance.

III. CLASSES OF SUBSTANCES SUBJECT TO CERTAIN RESTRICTIONS

A. Alcohol

When the regulations of a competent sports authority (sports federation or international federation) so provide, tests will be carried out for ethanol.

B. Cannabinoids

When the regulations of a competent national or international sports federation so provide, tests will be carried out for cannabinoids (such as marijuana and hashish).

C. Local anesthetics

Injectable local anesthetics are permitted under the following conditions:

- a) bupivacaine, lidocaine, mepivacaine, procaine and related substances may be used but not cocaine; vasoconstrictor agents may be used in conjunction with local anesthetics;
b) only local or intra-articular injections may be administered;
c) only when the administration is medically justified.

When the regulations of a competent sports authority so provide, it may be necessary to notify the administration of local anesthetics.

D. Glucocorticosteroids
The systemic use of glucocorticosteroids is prohibited when they are administered orally or rectally or by intravenous or intramuscular injection.

In case of medical necessity, local and intra-articular injections of glucocorticosteroids are permitted. When the regulations of a competent sports authority so provide, it may be necessary to notify the administration of glucocorticosteroids.

E. Beta-blockers

MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD 7047

The prohibited substances belonging to class (E) include the following examples: acebutolol, alprenolol, atenolol, labetalol, metoprolol, nadolol, oxprenolol, propranolol, sotalol, ... and related substances.

When the regulations of a competent sports authority so provide, tests will be carried out for beta-blockers.

IV. SUMMARY TABLE OF CONCENTRATIONS IN URINE ABOVE WHICH

THERE IS AN INFRACTION.

Caffeine > 12 micrograms/milliliter

Carboxy-THC > 15 nanograms/milliliter

Cathine > 5 micrograms/milliliter

Ephedrine > 10 micrograms/milliliter

Epitestosterone > 200 nanograms/milliliter

Methylephedrine > 10 micrograms/milliliter

Morphine > 1 microgram/milliliter

19-norandrosterone > 2 nanograms/milliliter in men
19-norandrosterone > 5 nanograms/milliliter in women

Phenylpropanolamine > 25 micrograms/milliliter

Pseudoephedrine > 25 micrograms/milliliter

Salbutamol (as anabolic agent) > 1000 nanograms/milliliter

T/E ratio* > 6

* Mentioned in sections I.C.b. and I.G.

LIST OF EXAMPLES OF PROHIBITED SUBSTANCES

WARNING: This is not an exhaustive list of prohibited substances. Many substances that are not listed in this list are considered prohibited under the designation "related substances".
Athletes must ensure that any medication, supplement, over-the-counter preparation or any other substance they use does not contain any prohibited substance.

STIMULANTS:

amfepramone, amiphenazole, amphetamine, bambuterol, bromantan, caffeine, carphedon, cathine, clobenzorex, cocaine, cropropamide, crotetamide, ephedrine, etamivan, etilamfetamine, etilefrine, fencamfamine, fenetylline, fenfluramine, fenproporex, formoterol, heptaminol, mefenorex, mephentermine, mesocarb, methamphetamine, methoxyphenamine, methylenedioxyamphetamine, methylenedioxymethamphetamine, methylephedrine, methylphenidate, nikethamide, norfenfluramine, parahydroxyamphetamine, pemoline, pentetrazol, phendimetrazine, phenmetrazine, phentermine, phenylpropanolamine, pholedrine, pipradol, prolintane, propylhexedrine, pseudoephedrine, reproterol, salbutamol, salmeterol, selegiline, strychnine, terbutaline.

NARCOTICS:

buprenorphine, dextromoramide, diamorphine (heroin), hydrocodone, methadone, morphine, pentazocine, pethidine.

ANABOLIC AGENTS:

androstenediol, androstenedione, bambuterol, bolasterone, boldenone, clenbuterol, clostebol, danazol, dehydrochloromethyltestosterone, dehydroepiandrosterone (DHEA), dihydrotestosterone, drostanolone, fenoterol, fluoxymesterone, formebolone, formoterol, gestrinone, mesterolone, methandienone, metenolone, methandriol, methyltestosterone, mibolerone, nandrolone, 19-norandrostenediol, 19-norandrostenedione, norbolethone, norethandrolone, oxandrolone, oxymesterone, oxymetholone, reproterol, salbutamol, salmeterol, stanozolol, terbutaline, testosterone, trenbolone.

DIURETICS:

amiloride, acetazolamide, ethacrynic acid, bendroflumethiazide, bumetanide, canrenone, chlorthalidone, furosemide, hydrochlorothiazide, indapamide, mannitol (by intravenous injection), mersalyl, spironolactone, triamterene.

MASKING AGENTS:

diuretics (see above), epitestosterone, probenecid, hydroxyethyl starch.

PEPTIDE HORMONES, MIMETIC SUBSTANCES AND ANALOGUES:

ACTH, erythropoietin (EPO), hCG*, hGH, insulin, LH*, IGF-1.

SUBSTANCES HAVING ANTI-ESTROGENIC ACTION

clomiphene*, cyclofenil*, tamoxifen*.

* Substances prohibited in men only.

BETA-BLOCKERS:

acebutolol, alprenolol, atenolol, betaxolol, bisoprolol, bunolol, carteolol, carvedilol, celiprolol, esmolol, labetalol, levobunolol, metipranolol, metoprolol, nadolol, oxprenolol, pindolol, propranolol, sotalol, timolol.
Seen to be annexed to the Government Order of December 5, 2002,
For the Government of the French Community,
The Minister of Youth Assistance and Health,
Mrs. N. MARECHAL

DECEMBER 5, 2002. — Order of the Government of the French Community amending the Order of the Government of the French Community of October 10, 2002 concerning the control procedure for doping practice and establishing the entry into force of certain provisions of the decree of

March 8, 2001 concerning the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and the prevention of doping in the French Community

The Government of the French Community,
Having regard to the decree of March 8, 2001 concerning the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and the prevention of doping in the French Community, in particular article 10;

Having regard to the Order of the Government of the French Community of October 10, 2002 concerning the control procedure for doping practice and establishing the entry into force of certain provisions of the decree of March 8, 2001 concerning the promotion of health in the practice of sport, the prohibition of doping and the prevention of doping in the French Community, in particular article 15;
Having regard to the opinion of the French-speaking Commission for the promotion of health in the practice of sport, given on November 22, 2002;

Having regard to the opinion of the High Council for Physical Education, Sport and Outdoor Life of the French Community, given on November 29, 2002;

Whereas there is urgent necessity because the new list of prohibited substances and methods of the International Olympic Committee enters into force on January 1, 2003, and because the list applicable in the French Community must be in conformity with that list;
On the proposal of the Minister with responsibility for health;
Having regard to the deliberation of the Government of the French Community of December 5, 2002;

Orders:

#### Article 1. The annex to the decree of the Government of the French Community of 10 October 2002 concerning

the control procedure for doping practices establishing the entry into force of certain provisions of the decree of 8 March 2001 concerning the promotion of health in sports practice, the doping ban and the prevention of doping in the French Community, is replaced by the annex attached to this decree.

#### Art. 2. This decree enters into force on 1 January 2003.

Brussels, 5 December 2002.

For the Government of the French Community, The Minister of Youth Assistance and Health, Mrs. N. MARECHAL

F. 2003 — 581 [C − 2003/29019]

5 DECEMBER 2002.—Decree of the Government of the French Community amending the decree of 8 February 2002 concerning the commission for health promotion at school The Government of the French Community, Having regard to the decree of 20 December 2001 concerning health promotion at school, in particular Article 28, §§ 1 and 9, amended by the decrees of 16 May and 20 June 2002; Having regard to the decree of the Government of the French Community of 8 February 2002 concerning the commission for health promotion at school;

Having regard to opinion No. 7 of the Commission for health promotion at school, given on 16 September 2002; Having regard to the opinion of the Finance Inspectorate, given on 25 September 2002; Having regard to the agreement of the Minister of Budget, given on 26 September 2002; Having regard to the deliberation of the Government of 26 September 2002 on the request for an opinion to be given by the Council of State within a period not exceeding one month; Having regard to opinion No. 34.212/4 of the Council of State, given on 19 November 2002, in application of Article 84, paragraph 1, 1°, of the coordinated laws on the Council of State;

On the proposal of the Minister with Health in her powers; Having regard to the deliberation of the Government of the French Community of 5 December 2002, Decrees:

#### Article 1. § 1. In Article 1, § 1, of the decree of 8 February 2002 concerning the commission for health promotion

at school, an article 5° bis is inserted, worded as follows: "5° bis. a head of establishment of ordinary or special secondary education of the French Community, or a head of establishment of ordinary or special fundamental education of the French Community, proposed by the competent Minister; the full member and the alternate shall be from a different level of education;"

MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD 7049

#### § 2. Article 1 of the same decree is completed with a § 3, worded as follows:

"When an opinion concerning health promotion in higher education is placed on the agenda of the commission, the commission is also composed of the following members:

1° an official of the General Service for Higher Education Institutions and higher-level artistic education, designated by the Secretary General of the Ministry of the French Community;

2° two representatives of organisations representing students at community level, chosen after consultation with these organisations."

#### Art. 2.

The Minister with Health in his powers is charged with the execution of this decree. Brussels, 5 December 2002.

For the Government of the French Community:

The Minister of Youth Assistance and Health, Mrs. N. MARECHAL

#### Article 28, §§ 1 and 9, amended by the decrees of 16 May and 20 June 2002;

Having regard to opinion No. 7 of the commission for health promotion at school, given on 16 September 2002;

Having regard to the opinion of the Finance Inspectorate, given on 25 September 2002; Having regard to the agreement of the Minister of Budget, given on 26 September 2002; Having regard to the deliberation of the Government of 26 September 2002 on the request for an opinion to be given by the Council of State within a period not exceeding one month; Having regard to opinion No. 34.212/4 of the Council of State, given on 19 November 2002, in application of Article 84, paragraph 1, 1° of the coordinated laws on the Council of State; On the proposal of the Minister with Health in his powers; Having regard to the deliberation of the Government of the French Community of 5 December 2002, Decrees:

F. 2003 — 583 [C − 2003/27104]

19 DECEMBER 2002.—Decree of the Walloon Government amending the decree of 6 March 1997 implementing the decree of 4 July 1996 relating to the integration of foreign persons or persons of foreign origin The Walloon Government, Having regard to the decree of 4 July 1996 relating to the integration of foreign persons or persons of foreign origin, the staff scales;

Having regard to the decree of 6 March 1997 implementing the decree of 4 July 1996 relating to the integration of foreign persons or persons of foreign origin;

Having regard to the opinion of the Finance Inspectorate;

Having regard to the agreement of the Minister of Budget;

Having regard to the collective labour agreement C of 16 September 2002 defining the classification of functions and the remuneration conditions for the sectors of the Joint Committee for the socio-cultural sector dependent on the Walloon Region: training-through-work enterprises, socio-professional integration organisations, regional integration centres for populations of foreign origin, regional missions for employment and vocational training and/or rehabilitation centres approved by the Walloon Agency for the Integration of Persons with Disabilities; Having regard to the laws on the Council of State, coordinated on 12 January 1973, in particular Article 3, §1er, amended by the laws of 4 July 1989 and 4 August 1996;

Having regard to the urgency specially motivated by the fact that, on the one hand, the collective labour agreement of 16 September 2002 provides, in its Article 8, for its retroactivity from 1 October 2001 in accordance with the non-commercial agreement and that, on the other hand, the centres are experiencing significant cash flow difficulties; On the proposal of the Minister of Social Affairs and Health;

After deliberation, Decrees:

#### Article 1er.

This decree settles, in application of Article 138 of the Constitution, a matter referred to in Article 128,

#### §1er, thereof.

#### Art. 2. Article 12 of the decree of the Walloon Government of 6 March 1997 implementing the decree of

4 July 1996 relating to the integration of foreign persons or persons of foreign origin is replaced by the following provision:

"The person in charge of day-to-day management must possess: 1° a university or non-university higher education diploma, of long or short type; 2° useful experience of at least five years and professional experience of at least three years; 3° a supplementary diploma or certificate attesting to knowledge acquired in the management of the non-commercial sector. The Minister determines the diplomas and certificates that satisfy this requirement. Useful experience includes experience in the sector and experience in the profession in the field of training provided.

Personnel expenses are only taken into consideration for the calculation of the subsidy if they do not exceed the salary scales relating to their function and annexed to this decree. The salary scale taken into account from 1 October 2004 for the person responsible for day-to-day management is the scale fixed in Annex 2.

MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD 7051

If the person responsible for day-to-day management was engaged after 1 October 2001, it is necessary, for the period from 1 October 2001 to 1 October 2004, to apply the scales set out in Annex 2bis. If the person responsible for day-to-day management was engaged before 1 October 2001, it is necessary to proceed, for the period from 1 October 2001 to 1 October 2004, to the calculation of the remuneration of said person responsible in accordance with the calculation grid determined by Annex 3 of the collective labour agreement defining the classification of functions and the remuneration conditions for certain sectors dependent on the Walloon Region of the Joint Committee for the socio-cultural sector: E.F.T., O.I.S.P., CRI, MIRE, C.F.P. of AWIPH signed on 16 September 2002. For the position of secretary, the salary scale taken into account from 1 October 2004 is fixed in Annex 3 or 4 depending on whether the worker holds an upper secondary education diploma or a lower secondary education diploma.

If the secretary was engaged after 1 October 2001, it is necessary, for the period from 1 October 2001 to 1 October 2004, to apply the scales set out in Annex 3bis or 4bis depending on whether the worker holds an upper secondary education diploma or a lower secondary education diploma.

If the secretary was engaged before 1 October 2001, it is necessary to proceed, for the period from 1 October 2001 to 1 October 2004, to the calculation of the remuneration of said secretary in accordance with the calculation grid determined by Annex 3 of the collective labour agreement defining the classification of functions and the remuneration conditions for certain sectors dependent on the Walloon Region of the Joint Committee for the socio-cultural sector: E.F.T., O.I.S.P., CRI, MIRE, C.F.P. of AWIPH signed on 16 September 2002. The salary scale and the annexed scales are intended to cover the gross salary to which it is appropriate to add the employer social charges capped at 50% of the gross salary."

#### Art. 3. Article 14 of the decree of the Walloon Government of 6 March 1997 implementing the decree of

4 July 1996 relating to the integration of foreign persons or persons of foreign origin is replaced by the following provision:

"With the exception of the subsidies referred to in Article 13, paragraph 3, the approved centre is granted, during the first quarter of the calendar year, an annual advance corresponding to 85% of the amount of subsidies granted the previous year.

To obtain payment of this advance, the service requests it by returning to the administration a form established by the latter.

The balance is liquidated before 1 June of the following year, upon presentation of supporting documents for the expenditure."

#### Art. 4.

Annex 2 is repealed and is replaced by Annexes 2, 2bis, 3, 3bis, 4 and 4bis of this decree.

#### Art. 5. Persons in charge of day-to-day management engaged before the date of entry into force of this

decree are presumed to meet the conditions fixed by Article 2 of this decree.

#### Art. 6.

Subject to paragraph 2, this decree enters into force on the day of its adoption by the Government.

Article 2 produces its effects on 1 October 2001.

#### Art. 7. The Minister of Social Affairs is charged with the execution of this decree.

Namur, 19 December 2002.

The Minister-President, J.-Cl. VANCAUWENBERGHE

The Minister of Social Affairs and Health, Th. DETIENNE

MINISTRY OF THE WALLOON REGION

N. 2003 — 583 [C − 2003/27104] 19 DECEMBER 2002.—Decree of the Walloon Government amending the decree of 6 March 1997 on the execution of the decree of 4 July 1996 concerning the integration of foreigners or persons of foreign origin

The Walloon Government, Having regard to the decree of 4 July 1996 concerning the integration of foreigners or persons of foreign origin; Having regard to the decree of 6 March 1997 on the execution of the decree of 4 July 1996 concerning the integration of foreigners or persons of foreign origin;

Having regard to the opinion of the Finance Inspectorate;

Having regard to the agreement of the Minister of Budget; Having regard to the collective labour agreement of 16 September 2002 concerning the classification of functions and remuneration conditions for the sectors of the joint committee for the socio-cultural sector falling under the Walloon Region, namely: the companies for training in the workplace, the institutions for social integration and integration into the labour process, the regional centres for the integration of persons of foreign origin, the regional missions for labour mediation and the centres for training and/or vocational retraining recognised by the Walloon Agency for the Vocational Integration of Disabled Persons; Having regard to the laws on the Council of State, coordinated on 12 January 1973, in particular Article 3, amended by the laws of 4 July 1989 and 4 August 1996; Having regard to the urgent necessity, specially motivated by the fact that the collective labour agreement of 16 September 2002 in its Article 8 provides that it has retroactive effect from 1 October 2001 under the non-profit agreement, on the one hand, and that the centres are facing serious treasury problems, on the other hand; On the proposal of the Minister of Social Affairs and Health;

After deliberation, Decrees:

#### Article 1.

This decree regulates, in accordance with Article 138 of the Constitution, a matter referred to in Article 128, §1, of the Constitution.

#### Art. 2. Article 12 of the decree of the Walloon Government of 6 March 1997 on the execution of the decree of

4 July 1996 concerning the integration of foreigners or persons of foreign origin is replaced as follows:

"The person charged with daily management has: 1° a diploma of university or non-university higher education of the long or short type; 2° at least five years of useful experience and at least three years of professional experience; 3° an additional diploma or certificate confirming the knowledge acquired in the field of management in the non-profit sector. The Minister determines the diplomas and certificates that meet those requirements. Useful experience means experience acquired in the sectors and professional experience in the field of the training given. Personnel expenditure is only taken into account in the calculation of the allowance if it does not exceed the salary scales attached to this decree. The salary scale taken into account from 1 October 2004 in the case of the person responsible for daily management is set out in Annex 2. If the person responsible for daily management was employed before 1 October 2001, the period between 1 October 2001 and 1 October 2004 falls under the application of the salary scales mentioned in Annex 2bis. If the person responsible for daily management was employed before 1 October 2001, their salary for the period between 1 October 2001 and 1 October 2004 is calculated in accordance with the method referred to in Annex 5 to the collective labour agreement of 16 September 2002 concerning the classification of functions and remuneration conditions for certain sectors of the joint committee of the socio-cultural sector falling under the Walloon Region, namely: the "E.F.T.", the "O.I.S.P.", the "CRI", the "MIRE", the "C.F.P." of the "AWIPH".

7056 MONITEUR BELGE — 12.02.2003 — BELGISCH STAATSBLAD

The salary scale concerning the post of secretary, which is taken into account from 1 October 2004, is set out in Annex 3 or 4, depending on whether the employee has a diploma of higher or lower secondary education.

If the secretary was employed before 1 October 2001, the period between 1 October 2001 and 1 October 2004 falls under the application of the salary scales referred to in Annex 3bis or 4bis, depending on whether the employee has a diploma of higher or lower secondary education.

If the secretary was employed before 1 October 2001, their salary for the period between 1 October 2001 and 1 October 2004 is calculated in accordance with the method referred to in Annex 5 to the collective labour agreement of 16 September 2002 concerning the classification of functions and remuneration conditions for certain sectors of the joint committee of the socio-cultural sector falling under the Walloon Region, namely: the "E.F.T.", the "O.I.S.P.", the "CRI", the "MIRE", the "C.F.P." of the "AWIPH". The salary scales and attached scales serve to cover the gross salary, to which employer charges must be added up to a maximum of 50% of the gross salary."

#### Art. 3. Article 14 of the decree of the Walloon Government of 6 March 1997 on the execution of the decree of

4 July 1996 concerning the integration of foreigners or persons of foreign origin is replaced as follows: "With the exception of the allowances referred to in Article 13, third paragraph, the recognised centre receives during the first quarter of the calendar year an annual advance equal to 85% of the amount of the allowances granted during the previous year. The service requests the administration for payment of that advance by means of a form, the model of which is determined by the administration.

The balance is settled before 1 June of the following year on presentation of the documents confirming the expenditure."

#### Art. 4.

Annex 2 is repealed and replaced by Annexes 2, 2bis, 3, 3bis, 4 and 4bis of this decree.

#### Art. 5. Persons charged with daily management who are employed before the entry into force of

this decree are deemed to meet the conditions set out in Article 2 of this decree.

#### Art. 6. This decree enters into force on the day it is adopted by the Government, subject to

the second paragraph.

#### Art. 7. The Minister of Social Affairs is responsible for the implementation of this decree.

Namur, 19 December 2002.

The Minister-President, J.-Cl. VANCAUWENBERGHE

The Minister of Social Affairs and Health, Th. DETIENNE
